5CCB: BASIC Program
Kentilla by Derek Brewster, published by Micromega in 1984. It is the sequel to Velnor's Lair in his "Myths and Legends of Caraland" pair; see also Reviewed With His Own Ratings.
The game is driven from BASIC. After the custom tape loader has finished, line 113 prints What now? and calls into the support code with LET A=USR JumpTable_InputLine and LET A=USR JumpTable_AdvanceInputPointer. Verb and command indices from JumpTable / JumpTable_SearchVerbs / JumpTable_SearchCommands are dispatched in BASIC (see JumpTable).
The original Micromega tape uses a REM bootstrap at 5CD0 and a Micromega 1.1 loader at LoadedMachineCode; both are overwritten by the time this snapshot is taken. See the Loading Code page.
